        .sidebar-header { padding: 16px 20px; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08); display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; }
        .sidebar-header .left-group { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }
        .sidebar-header h2 { font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; }
        .sidebar-header .theme-toggle { background: transparent; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.2); color: white; padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 20px; font-size: 14px; cursor: pointer; transition: .2s; }
        .sidebar-header .theme-toggle:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); }
        .sidebar-header .dropdown-tabs { position: relative; display: inline-block; }
        .sidebar-header .dropdown-tabs .dropbtn { background: rgba(255,255,255,.08); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.15); color: white; padding: 6px 16px; border-radius: 20px; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 500; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; }
        .sidebar-header .dropdown-tabs .dropbtn:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.15); }
        .sidebar-header .dropdown-tabs .dropdown-content { display: none; position: absolute; right: 0; top: 100%; background: rgba(13,28,41,.95); backdrop-filter: blur(12px); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.12); border-radius: 12px; min-width: 140px; box-shadow: 0 15px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.4); z-index: 100; padding: 6px 0; margin-top: 6px; }
        .sidebar-header .dropdown-tabs .dropdown-content button { background: transparent; border: none; color: #bdd0df; padding: 10px 20px; width: 100%; text-align: left; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; cursor: pointer; transition: .2s; }
        .sidebar-header .dropdown-tabs .dropdown-content button:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); color: white; }
        .sidebar-header .dropdown-tabs .dropdown-content button.active { color: #00d4ff; background: rgba(0,212,255,.05); }
        .sidebar-header .dropdown-tabs.show .dropdown-content { display: block; }

        .sidebar-search { padding: 12px 20px; display: flex; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
        .sidebar-search input { flex: 1; padding: 10px 16px; border-radius: 12px; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.1); background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); color: white; font-size: 14px; outline: none; transition: .3s; min-width: 120px; }
        .sidebar-search input:focus { border-color: #00d4ff; }
        .sidebar-search input::placeholder { color: #9fb5c8; }
        .sidebar-search .create-group-btn { padding: 10px 16px; background: rgba(0,212,255,.15); border: 1px solid rgba(0,212,255,.3); border-radius: 12px; color: #00d4ff; cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600; transition: .2s; white-space: nowrap; }
        .sidebar-search .create-group-btn:hover { background: rgba(0,212,255,.25); }

        .dialogs-list, .news-feed { flex: 1; overflow-y: auto; padding: 8px 0; }
        .dialog-item { display: flex; align-items: center; padding: 12px 20px; cursor: pointer; transition: .2s; border-left: 3px solid transparent; gap: 12px; }
        .dialog-item:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); }
        .dialog-item.active { background: rgba(0,212,255,.08); border-left-color: #00d4ff; }
        .dialog-avatar { width: 44px; height: 44px; border-radius: 50%; background: rgba(0,212,255,.2); display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 600; color: #00d4ff; flex-shrink: 0; position: relative; }
        .dialog-avatar.group { background: rgba(255,200,0,.2); color: #ffc800; }
        .dialog-avatar .online-dot { width: 10px; height: 10px; border-radius: 50%; background: #00ffa3; border: 2px solid #08131d; position: absolute; bottom: 0; right: 0; }
        .dialog-info { flex: 1; min-width: 0; }
        .dialog-info .name { font-weight: 600; font-size: 15px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
        .dialog-info .last-msg { font-size: 13px; color: #9fb5c8; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
        .dialog-time { font-size: 11px; color: #6b7f94; flex-shrink: 0; }
        .unread-badge { background: #00d4ff; color: #08131d; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; padding: 2px 8px; border-radius: 20px; min-width: 20px; text-align: center; }

        .news-item { padding: 16px 20px; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.06); cursor: pointer; transition: .15s; }
        .news-item:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.03); }
        .news-item .title { font-weight: 600; font-size: 16px; margin-bottom: 4px; }
        .news-item .summary { font-size: 14px; color: #bdd0df; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 2; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden; }
        .news-item .meta { font-size: 12px; color: #9fb5c8; margin-top: 6px; display: flex; gap: 12px; }
        .news-item .image-placeholder { margin-top: 8px; background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); border-radius: 12px; padding: 20px; text-align: center; font-size: 40px; color: #9fb5c8; }

        .messenger-chat { flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column; background: rgba(0,0,0,.1); }
        .chat-header { padding: 16px 24px; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px; background: rgba(0,0,0,.15); min-height: 70px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
        .chat-header .back-btn { display: none; background: transparent; border: none; color: white; font-size: 20px; cursor: pointer; }
        .chat-header .chat-avatar { width: 40px; height: 40px; border-radius: 50%; background: rgba(0,212,255,.2); display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 18px; font-weight: 600; color: #00d4ff; }
        .chat-header .chat-name { font-weight: 700; font-size: 18px; }
        .chat-header .chat-status { font-size: 13px; color: #9fb5c8; }
        .chat-header .chat-actions { margin-left: auto; display: flex; gap: 6px; }
        .chat-header .chat-actions button { width: 34px; height: 34px; font-size: 15px; border-radius: 50%; background: transparent; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.15); color: #9fb5c8; cursor: pointer; transition: .2s; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; }
        .chat-header .chat-actions button:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); border-color: #00d4ff; color: white; }

        .chat-messages { flex: 1; overflow-y: auto; padding: 20px 24px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px; }
        .message { max-width: 75%; padding: 12px 18px; border-radius: 18px; font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.5; word-wrap: break-word; position: relative; }
        .message.sent { align-self: flex-end; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #00d4ff, #008cff); color: white; border-bottom-right-radius: 6px; }
        .message.received { align-self: flex-start; background: rgba(255,255,255,.08); color: white; border-bottom-left-radius: 6px; }
        .message .msg-time { font-size: 11px; color: rgba(255,255,255,.5); margin-top: 4px; text-align: right; }
        .message.received .msg-time { color: #9fb5c8; }
        .message .msg-username { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; color: #00d4ff; margin-bottom: 4px; }
        .message .file-attachment { display: inline-block; background: rgba(255,255,255,.1); padding: 6px 12px; border-radius: 8px; margin-top: 6px; font-size: 13px; }
        .message .file-attachment a { color: #00d4ff; text-decoration: none; }

        .typing-indicator { align-self: flex-start; font-size: 14px; color: #9fb5c8; padding: 8px 16px; background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); border-radius: 18px; animation: pulse 1.5s infinite; }
        @keyframes pulse { 0%, 100% { opacity: 0.6; } 50% { opacity: 1; } }

        .chat-input-area { padding: 16px 24px; border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08); display: flex; gap: 10px; background: rgba(0,0,0,.1); align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; }
        .chat-input-area .input-group { flex: 1; display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; min-width: 200px; }
        .chat-input-area .input-group input { flex: 1; padding: 12px 18px; border-radius: 24px; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.1); background: rgba(255,255,255,.05); color: white; font-size: 15px; outline: none; transition: .3s; min-width: 120px; }
        .chat-input-area .input-group input:focus { border-color: #00d4ff; }
        .chat-input-area .input-group input::placeholder { color: #9fb5c8; }
        .chat-input-area .input-group .emoji-btn, .chat-input-area .input-group .file-btn { background: transparent; border: none; font-size: 22px; cursor: pointer; transition: .2s; padding: 4px 6px; color: #9fb5c8; }
        .chat-input-area .input-group .emoji-btn:hover, .chat-input-area .input-group .file-btn:hover { color: white; }
        .chat-input-area .call-buttons { display: flex; gap: 6px; }
        .chat-input-area .call-buttons button { width: 38px; height: 38px; font-size: 18px; border-radius: 50%; background: transparent; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.2); color: white; cursor: pointer; transition: .2s; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; }
        .chat-input-area .call-buttons button:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.1); border-color: #00d4ff; }
        .chat-input-area .send-btn { padding: 12px 24px; border: none; border-radius: 24px; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #00d4ff, #008cff); color: white; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; transition: .3s; }
        .chat-input-area .send-btn:hover { transform: scale(1.02); box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,212,255,.3); }
        .chat-input-area .send-btn:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed; }

        .emoji-picker { display: none; position: absolute; bottom: 70px; left: 20px; background: rgba(13,28,41,.95); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.12); border-radius: 16px; padding: 16px; width: 280px; max-height: 200px; overflow-y: auto; backdrop-filter: blur(12px); z-index: 200; box-shadow: 0 15px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.4); }
        .emoji-picker.active { display: block; }
        .emoji-picker .emoji-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(7, 1fr); gap: 6px; }
        .emoji-picker .emoji-grid button { background: transparent; border: none; font-size: 24px; cursor: pointer; padding: 4px; transition: .15s; border-radius: 8px; }
        .emoji-picker .emoji-grid button:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,.1); }

        /* ===== МОДАЛЬНОЕ ОКНО ГРУППЫ И ЗВОНКОВ (стеклянный дизайн) ===== */
        .group-modal, .call-modal {
            display: none;
            position: fixed;
            top: 0; left: 0;
            width: 100%; height: 100%;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,.7);
            backdrop-filter: blur(15px);
            -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(15px);
            justify-content: center;
            align-items: center;
            z-index: 1000;
            animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease;
        }
        .group-modal.active, .call-modal.active { display: flex; }

        .group-modal .modal-box, .call-modal .modal-box {
            background: rgba(20,30,45,0.92);
            backdrop-filter: blur(35px);
            -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(35px);
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.18);
            border-radius: 24px;
            padding: 30px 35px;
            max-width: 420px;
            width: 95%;
            box-shadow: 0 30px 80px rgba(0,0,0,.6);
            position: relative;
            text-align: center;
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box::before, .call-modal .modal-box::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0; left: 0; right: 0;
            height: 2px;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, #00d4ff, #00ffa3, transparent);
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box h2, .call-modal .modal-box h2 {
            font-size: 24px;
            font-weight: 700;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            background: linear-gradient(to right, #fff, #b4d7ff);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-group { margin-bottom: 16px; text-align: left; }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-group label {
            display: block;
            font-size: 14px;
            color: #dbe9ff;
            margin-bottom: 4px;
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-group input,
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-group select {
            width: 100%;
            padding: 12px 16px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.15);
            background: rgba(255,255,255,.08);
            color: white;
            font-size: 15px;
            outline: none;
            transition: .3s;
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-group input:focus,
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-group select:focus {
            border-color: #00d4ff;
            box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(0,212,255,.2);
            background: rgba(255,255,255,.12);
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-group select option { background: #0b2035; color: white; }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-actions, .call-actions {
            display: flex;
            gap: 12px;
            justify-content: center;
            margin-top: 20px;
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-actions button, .call-actions button {
            padding: 12px 28px;
            border: none;
            border-radius: 12px;
            font-weight: 600;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: .3s;
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-actions .btn-create {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #00d4ff, #008cff);
            color: white;
            box-shadow: 0 10px 25px rgba(0,212,255,.35);
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-actions .btn-create:hover {
            transform: scale(1.02);
            box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(0,212,255,.5);
        }
        .group-modal .modal-box .form-actions .btn-cancel, .btn-end-call {
            background: rgba(255, 77, 77, 0.2);
            border: 1px solid rgba(255, 77, 77, 0.4);
            color: #ff6b6b;
        }
        .btn-end-call:hover {
            background: rgba(255, 77, 77, 0.4);
            color: #fff;
        }

        /* Звонки стили */
        .call-avatar { width: 90px; height: 90px; border-radius: 50%; background: rgba(0,212,255,.2); color: #00d4ff; font-size: 36px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; margin: 0 auto 16px; border: 2px solid #00d4ff; animation: pulse 2s infinite; }
        .call-status { color: #9fb5c8; font-size: 14px; margin-top: 4px; }

        @keyframes fadeIn {
            from { opacity: 0; transform: scale(0.95); }
            to { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1); }
        }

        .empty-chat { flex: 1; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; flex-direction: column; color: #9fb5c8; text-align: center; padding: 40px; }
        .empty-chat .icon { font-size: 60px; margin-bottom: 20px; opacity: 0.5; }
        .empty-chat h3 { font-size: 22px; color: white; margin-bottom: 8px; }

        .empty-state { text-align: center; padding: 30px 20px; color: #9fb5c8; }
        .empty-state .icon { font-size: 40px; margin-bottom: 10px; }

        /* ===== ТОСТЫ ===== */
        .toast { position: fixed; bottom: 30px; right: 30px; background: rgba(13,28,41,.9); backdrop-filter: blur(10px); border: 1px solid rgba(0,212,255,.3); border-radius: 16px; padding: 14px 24px; color: white; font-weight: 500; box-shadow: 0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.4); z-index: 2000; animation: slideUp 0.3s ease; }
        .toast.success { border-left: 4px solid #00ffa3; }
        .toast.error { border-left: 4px solid #ff6b6b; }
        .toast.info { border-left: 4px solid #00d4ff; }
        @keyframes slideUp { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(30px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }

        /* Стеклянная шапка */
        header { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06) !important; backdrop-filter: blur(20px) !important; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08) !important; box-shadow: 0 4px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1) !important; }
        .header-right .lang, .header-right .logout-btn, .header-right .btn-switch { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08) !important; border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15) !important; color: #fff !important; backdrop-filter: blur(4px) !important; transition: all 0.3s !important; }
        .header-right .lang:hover, .header-right .logout-btn:hover, .header-right .btn-switch:hover { background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15) !important; border-color: #00d4ff !important; color: #00d4ff !important; }
        .logo-text p { display: none !important; }

        @media (max-width: 992px) { .messenger-sidebar { width: 260px; min-width: 220px; } }
        @media (max-width: 768px) {
            .messenger-container { flex-direction: column; height: auto; min-height: 600px; }
            .messenger-sidebar { width: 100%; min-width: unset; border-right: none; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08); max-height: 300px; }
            .chat-header .back-btn { display: block; }
            .message { max-width: 90%; }
            .chat-input-area { flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: center; }
            .chat-input-area .input-group input { min-width: 100%; }
            .emoji-picker { left: 10px; width: 250px; }
        }
        .header-right .lang,
.header-right .logout-btn,
.header-right .btn-switch {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 8px 16px;
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
    color: #fff;
    font-size: 14px;
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: all .3s;
}
.header-right .lang:hover,
.header-right .logout-btn:hover,
.header-right .btn-switch:hover {
    background: rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
    border-color: #00d4ff;
    color: #00d4ff;
}
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div id="particles"></div>

    <header>
        <div class="container">
            <a href="index.html" class="logo">
                <div class="logo-icon">
                    <svg class="logo-svg" viewBox="0 0 100 100" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
                        <defs><linearGradient id="logoGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#00ffa3"/><stop offset="50%" stop-color="#00d4ff"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#1f6bff"/></linearGradient></defs>
                        <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="28" fill="url(#logoGrad)" opacity="0.15"/>
                        <path d="M50 12 C72 12, 88 28, 88 50 C88 72, 68 88, 46 86 C26 84, 14 66, 16 46 C18 28, 34 16, 52 18 C68 20, 78 34, 76 50 C74 64, 62 74, 48 72 C36 70, 28 58, 30 46 C32 36, 42 28, 52 30 C60 32, 66 40, 64 50 C62 58, 54 62, 46 60" stroke="url(#logoGrad)" stroke-width="3"/>
                        <circle cx="48" cy="48" r="3.5" fill="#ffffff"/>
                    </svg>
                </div>
                <div class="logo-text"><h2>SFERA</h2><p id="logoSubtitle" data-i18n="logo_subtitle">Milli Sanly Platforma</p></div>
            </a>
            <div class="header-right">
                <span class="user-info">👤 <strong id="username">User</strong></span>
                <button class="lang" id="langBtn">RU</button>
                <button class="logout-btn" id="logoutBtn" data-i18n="logout">🚪 Çykmak</button>
                <a href="#" id="backLink" class="btn-switch" data-i18n="back">← Yza</a>
            </div>
        </div>
    </header>

    <section class="messenger-page">
        <div class="container" style="flex:1; display:flex; flex-direction:column;">
            <div class="messenger-container" id="messengerContainer">
                <div class="messenger-sidebar" id="sidebar">
                    <div class="sidebar-header">
                        <div class="left-group">
                            <h2 data-i18n="messenger_title">Söhbet</h2>
                            <button class="theme-toggle" id="themeToggle" data-i18n="theme_toggle">🌙</button>
                        </div>
                        <div class="dropdown-tabs" id="tabsDropdown">
                            <button class="dropbtn" id="dropbtn">📋 <span id="currentTabLabel" data-i18n="tab_chats">Çatlar</span> ▾</button>
                            <div class="dropdown-content" id="dropdownContent">
                                <button class="active" data-tab="chats" data-i18n="tab_chats">Çatlar</button>
                                <button data-tab="news" data-i18n="tab_news">Habarlar</button>
                                <button data-tab="assistant" data-i18n="tab_assistant">Kömekçi</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="sidebar-search">
                        <input type="text" id="searchInput" placeholder="Gözleg..." data-i18n-placeholder="search_placeholder" />
                        <button class="create-group-btn" id="createGroupBtn" data-i18n="create_group">➕ Topar</button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="dialogs-list" id="dialogsList">
                        <div class="empty-state" id="loadingDialogs" style="padding:30px 20px; text-align:center; color:#9fb5c8;">
                            <div class="icon">⏳</div>
                            <p data-i18n="loading">Ýüklenýär...</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="news-feed" id="newsFeed" style="display:none;"></div>
                </div>

                <div class="messenger-chat" id="chatWindow">
                    <div class="chat-header" id="chatHeader">
                        <button class="back-btn" id="backBtn">←</button>
                        <div class="chat-avatar" id="chatAvatar">👤</div>
                        <div>
                            <div class="chat-name" id="chatName" data-i18n="select_chat">Söhbet</div>
                            <div class="chat-status" id="chatStatus" data-i18n="online">onlaýn</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="chat-actions">
                            <button id="searchInChatBtn" data-i18n-title="search_chat" title="🔍">🔍</button>
                            <button id="exportChatBtn" data-i18n-title="export_chat" title="📥">📥</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="chat-messages" id="chatMessages">
                        <div class="empty-chat">
                            <div class="icon">💬</div>
                            <h3 data-i18n="select_chat">Söhbeti saýlaň</h3>
                            <p data-i18n="select_chat_desc">Çatdaşlaryňyzdan birini saýlaň ýa-da täze söhbet başlaň.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="chat-input-area" id="chatInputArea">
                        <div class="input-group">
                            <button class="emoji-btn" id="emojiBtn">😊</button>
                            <button class="file-btn" id="fileBtn">📎</button>
                            <input type="file" id="fileInput" style="display:none;" multiple accept="image/*,.pdf,.doc,.docx,.txt" />
                            <input type="text" id="messageInput" placeholder="Habaryňyzy ýazyň..." data-i18n-placeholder="type_message" />
                        </div>
                        <div class="call-buttons">
                            <button id="audioCallBtn" data-i18n-title="audio_call" title="📞">📞</button>
                            <button id="videoCallBtn" data-i18n-title="video_call" title="📹">📹</button>
                        </div>
                        <button class="send-btn" id="sendBtn" data-i18n="send">Iber</button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="emoji-picker" id="emojiPicker">
                        <div class="emoji-grid" id="emojiGrid"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- Модальное окно создания группы -->
    <div class="group-modal" id="groupModal">
        <div class="modal-box">
            <h2 data-i18n="create_group_title">Täze topar döret</h2>
            <form id="groupForm">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="groupName" data-i18n="group_name">Toparyň ady</label>
                    <input type="text" id="groupName" placeholder="Mysal: Taslama topary" required />
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="groupMembers" data-i18n="group_members">Agzalar (çatdaşlary saýlaň)</label>
                    <select id="groupMembers" multiple style="height:100px;"></select>
                </div>
                <div class="form-actions">
                    <button type="button" class="btn-cancel" id="cancelGroupBtn" data-i18n="cancel">Ýatyr</button>
                    <button type="submit" class="btn-create" data-i18n="create">Döret</button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Модальное окно для Аудио / Видео звонков -->
    <div class="call-modal" id="callModal">
        <div class="modal-box">
            <div class="call-avatar" id="callAvatar">👤</div>
            <h2 id="callTargetName">Сердар Аннаев</h2>
            <div class="call-status" id="callStatusText">Идет вызов...</div>
            <div class="call-actions">
                <button class="btn-end-call" id="endCallBtn">Завершить</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

# 8. ИНСТРУКЦИЯ ДЛЯ НОВОГО ЧАТА

Используй этот файл как актуальный технический снимок проекта SFERA.

1. Сохраняй текущую архитектуру проекта.
2. Не переписывай архитектуру без прямого указания.
3. Не меняй утверждённый frontend-дизайн без прямого указания.
4. Перед изменением существующих файлов учитывай их текущее содержимое.
5. Используй Git-состояние и историю выше как источник текущего состояния разработки.
6. Не считай старые решения актуальными, если текущие файлы и последние коммиты им противоречат.
7. При продолжении разработки сначала определи текущую точку проекта по этому снимку.
8. Не удаляй существующий функционал только ради упрощения кода.
